#### `double recallAtK(const std::vector< std::string > &ranked_ids, const std::vector< std::string > &relevant_ids, size_t k)`
- Source: `benchmarks/rag/bench_rag_evaluation.cpp`:86
- Brief: Compute recall@K given a ranked list of doc IDs and a relevant set.
- Parameters:
  - `ranked_ids` (const std::vector< std::string > &): Retrieved document IDs in descending score order.
  - `relevant_ids` (const std::vector< std::string > &): Set of known-relevant document IDs.
  - `k` (size_t): Cut-off rank.
- Details: recall@K = \|retrieved_top_K ∩ relevant\| / \|relevant\| ranked_ids Retrieved document IDs in descending score order. relevant_ids Set of known-relevant document IDs. k Cut-off rank.

### test_rag_highcardinality_stress.cpp

#### `TEST(RAGHighCardinalityStress, ConcurrentQueryStress)`
- Source: `tests/rag/test_rag_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:194
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (RAGHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (ConcurrentQueryStress): n/a
- Details: TestConcurrentQueryStress Fires 8 concurrent threads each issuing 5 000 queries against a shared 50 000-chunk stub index and asserts zero query failures and that aggregate throughput exceeds 50 000 queries/sec (well above the 500 qps soak gate).

#### `TEST(RAGHighCardinalityStress, HighCardinalityChunkIndex)`
- Source: `tests/rag/test_rag_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:124
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (RAGHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (HighCardinalityChunkIndex): n/a
- Details: TestHighCardinalityChunkIndex Builds a 100 000-chunk stub index using 8 writer threads, then validates query correctness from 8 reader threads. Verifies that the index size reaches exactly 100 000 after concurrent build.

#### `TEST(RAGHighCardinalityStress, LLMJudgeCachePressure)`
- Source: `tests/rag/test_rag_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:263
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (RAGHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (LLMJudgeCachePressure): n/a
- Details: TestLLMJudgeCachePressure Exercises the stub judge cache under eviction pressure: inserts 10× the cache capacity and verifies that every cached verdict can be retrieved correctly within the current window (no incorrect values returned).

### themis::rag

#### `std::vector< std::vector< knowledge_gap::RetrievedDocument > > batchConvertToRetrievedDocuments(const std::vector< std::string > &queries, VectorIndexManager &vector_mgr, RocksDBWrapper &db, const EmbeddingFunction &embed_fn, size_t k=10, VectorIndexManager::Metric metric=VectorIndexManager::Metric::COSINE)`
- Source: `include/rag/rag_integration_helpers.h`:196
- Brief: Batch conversion for multiple queries.
- Parameters:
  - `queries` (const std::vector< std::string > &): Vector of query strings to process.
  - `vector_mgr` (VectorIndexManager &): VectorIndexManager instance (must outlive this call).
  - `db` (RocksDBWrapper &): Database wrapper for entity content retrieval.
  - `embed_fn` (const EmbeddingFunction &): Function that converts a query string to a float embedding. Called sequentially from a single thread; no concurrency guarantees are required of the provided callable.
  - `k` (size_t): Number of nearest neighbours to retrieve per query.
  - `metric` (VectorIndexManager::Metric): Distance metric used for similarity conversion.
- Return: Vector of document lists — one per input query (same order). An empty inner list indicates that either embedding generation failed or the K-NN search returned no results for that query.
- Details: For each query string the provided embed_fn generates a dense embedding, which is then used for a K-NN search via vector_mgr. Resulting VectorIndexManager::Result lists are converted to RetrievedDocument vectors using convertToRetrievedDocuments(). queries Vector of query strings to process. vector_mgr VectorIndexManager instance (must outlive this call). db Database wrapper for entity content retrieval. embed_fn Function that converts a query string to a float embedding. Called sequentially from a single thread; no concurrency guarantees are required of the provided callable. k Number of nearest neighbours to retrieve per query. metric Distance metric used for similarity conversion. Vector of document lists — one per input query (same order). An empty inner list indicates that either embedding generation failed or the K-NN search returned no results for that query. This function processes queries sequentially. Parallel execution requires a thread-safe VectorIndexManager implementation.

#### `float bm25PlusScore(const std::vector< std::string > &query_terms, const std::string &doc_text, float avg_doc_len, const std::unordered_map< std::string, float > &idf_map)`
- Source: `src/rag/wiki_index_store.cpp`:88
- Brief: Compute BM25+ score for a single document against a set of query terms.
- Parameters:
  - `query_terms` (const std::vector< std::string > &): Tokenised query terms (duplicates are summed).
  - `doc_text` (const std::string &): Raw document text (whitespace-tokenised internally).
  - `avg_doc_len` (float): Corpus average document length in tokens.
  - `idf_map` (const std::unordered_map< std::string, float > &): Pre-computed IDF values per term. Missing terms → IDF 0.
- Return: BM25+ relevance score (non-negative).
- Details: BM25+ formula per term t: score(t,d) = IDF(t) * [ (tf(t,d) * (k1+1)) / (tf(t,d) + k1*(1-b+b*dl/avgdl)) + delta ] Parameters: k1 = 1.5, b = 0.75, delta = 0.5 (BM25+ lower-bound correction). query_terms Tokenised query terms (duplicates are summed). doc_text Raw document text (whitespace-tokenised internally). avg_doc_len Corpus average document length in tokens. idf_map Pre-computed IDF values per term. Missing terms → IDF 0. BM25+ relevance score (non-negative).

#### `std::vector< knowledge_gap::RetrievedDocument > convertToRetrievedDocuments(const std::vector< VectorIndexManager::Result > &search_results, RocksDBWrapper &db, VectorIndexManager::Metric metric=VectorIndexManager::Metric::COSINE)`
- Source: `include/rag/rag_integration_helpers.h`:37
- Brief: Convert VectorIndexManager search results to RetrievedDocuments.
- Parameters:
  - `search_results` (const std::vector< VectorIndexManager::Result > &): Vector of search results from VectorIndexManager
  - `db` (RocksDBWrapper &): Database wrapper to retrieve full entity content
  - `metric` (VectorIndexManager::Metric): Distance metric used (affects similarity conversion)
- Return: Vector of RetrievedDocument structures
- Details: This helper converts search results from VectorIndexManager::searchKnn() into the format expected by KnowledgeGapDetector. search_results Vector of search results from VectorIndexManager db Database wrapper to retrieve full entity content metric Distance metric used (affects similarity conversion) Vector of RetrievedDocument structures For COSINE metric: similarity = 1 - distance For L2 metric: similarity = 1 / (1 + distance) For DOT metric: similarity = distance (assuming normalized vectors)

  - `ranked_lists` (const std::vector< std::vector< std::string > > &): Input parameter.
  - `k` (int): Input parameter.
- Return: Merged list sorted by descending RRF score.
- Details: Rrf Fusion. RRF(d) = Σ_r [ 1 / (k + rank_r(d)) ] where k = 60 (default). ranked_lists Each inner vector is an ordered list of doc_ids (most relevant first). k RRF constant (higher k reduces the penalty for low ranks). Merged list sorted by descending RRF score. ranked_lists Input parameter. k Input parameter. Return value.

#### `bool loadModel(const std::string &model_path)`
- Source: `include/rag/reranker.h`:199
- Brief: Load (or replace) the ONNX cross-encoder model.
- Parameters:
  - `model_path` (const std::string &): Path to the model.
- Return: true when the model passed validation and was loaded; false on invalid paths, checksum mismatches, or other integrity/safety failures.
- Details: Load Model. The loader canonicalises model_path, rejects symlinks, requires a regular .onnx file, validates bounded file size and permissions, and computes a SHA-256 digest before accepting the model. When a <model_path>.sha256 sidecar exists, the digest must match; when the sidecar is absent, the loader emits a warning and proceeds. model_path Path to the ONNX model file. true when the model passed validation and was loaded; false on invalid paths, checksum mismatches, or other integrity/safety failures. model_path Path to the model. True when the operation succeeds. Calls: empty(), THEMIS_WARN(), input_path(), std::filesystem::weakly_canonical(), THEMIS_ERROR(), std::filesystem::is_symlink(), extension(), verifyModelFile().

#### `RerankResult rerank(const std::string &query, const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &candidates, size_t top_k=0) const`
- Source: `include/rag/reranker.h`:153
- Brief: Re-rank candidate documents for a given query.
- Parameters:
  - `query` (const std::string &): Natural-language query.
  - `candidates` (const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &): Initial retrieval results (any order, any count).
  - `top_k` (size_t): Number of documents to return; 0 uses config default.
- Return: Re-rank result with sorted documents and score details.
- Details: Scores every candidate against the query, optionally reuses the internal score cache for repeated (query, document-id) pairs, and returns the top-k results sorted in descending relevance order. The similarity_score field of each returned document is replaced with the cross-encoder relevance score. Empty input, oversized queries/documents, or excessive candidate counts are rejected fail-closed and return an empty result instead of attempting partial scoring. query Natural-language query. candidates Initial retrieval results (any order, any count). top_k Number of documents to return; 0 uses config default. Re-rank result with sorted documents and score details.

#### `HybridFusionResult fuse(const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &bm25_candidates, const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &vector_candidates) const`
- Source: `include/rag/hybrid_retriever.h`:150
- Brief: Fuse BM25 and vector candidate lists into a single ranked list.
- Parameters:
  - `bm25_candidates` (const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &): BM25 / sparse-retrieval results in any order. The similarity_score field is used as the raw BM25 score when use_rrf = false.
  - `vector_candidates` (const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &): Vector / dense-retrieval results in any order. The similarity_score field is used as the raw vector score when use_rrf = false.
- Return: Fused result with documents sorted by descending hybrid score.
- Details: Either or both candidate lists may be empty; fusion degrades gracefully to the non-empty list in that case. The similarity_score field of every returned document is set to the fused hybrid score. bm25_candidates BM25 / sparse-retrieval results in any order. The similarity_score field is used as the raw BM25 score when use_rrf = false. vector_candidates Vector / dense-retrieval results in any order. The similarity_score field is used as the raw vector score when use_rrf = false. Fused result with documents sorted by descending hybrid score.

#### `HybridFusionResult retrieveWithVectorizer(const std::string &query, const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &bm25_candidates) const`
- Source: `include/rag/hybrid_retriever.h`:184
- Brief: Build dense candidates via configured IVectorizer and fuse them with BM25.
- Parameters:
  - `query` (const std::string &): Query text to encode.
  - `bm25_candidates` (const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &): BM25/sparse candidates (input pool for dense scoring).
- Return: Fused hybrid result.
- Throws:
  - std::invalid_argument: if query is empty.
  - std::runtime_error: if no vectorizer is configured or not initialized.
- Details: This is an integration helper for DPR-style bi-encoders: query text is encoded via encodeQuery(), each BM25 candidate content is encoded via encodePassage(), cosine similarity is used as dense score, then standard fuse() is applied. query Query text to encode. bm25_candidates BM25/sparse candidates (input pool for dense scoring). Fused hybrid result. std::invalid_argument if query is empty. std::runtime_error if no vectorizer is configured or not initialized.

#### `MultiStepRAGResult runIterative(const std::string &query, const std::vector< RetrievedChunk > &documents, const InferenceFn &infer, const RetrievalFn &retrieve={}) const`
- Source: `include/rag/multi_step_rag.h`:180
- Brief: Run Iterative RAG with context accumulation.
- Parameters:
  - `query` (const std::string &): Original user query.
  - `documents` (const std::vector< RetrievedChunk > &): Initial set of retrieved documents.
  - `infer` (const InferenceFn &): LLM inference callback (generation + gap detection).
  - `retrieve` (const RetrievalFn &): Retrieval callback for follow-up queries (may be {}).
- Return: MultiStepRAGResult with final_answer and iteration history.
- Details: Starts with the provided documents, generates an initial answer, then iteratively identifies uncovered query aspects, retrieves new documents, and refines the answer. Stops when the model reports no open aspects or max_iterations is reached. query Original user query. documents Initial set of retrieved documents. infer LLM inference callback (generation + gap detection). retrieve Retrieval callback for follow-up queries (may be {}). MultiStepRAGResult with final_answer and iteration history.

#### `MultiStepRAGResult runMapReduce(const std::string &query, const std::vector< RetrievedChunk > &documents, const InferenceFn &infer) const`
- Source: `include/rag/multi_step_rag.h`:159
- Brief: Run Map-Reduce RAG.
- Parameters:
  - `query` (const std::string &): Original user query.
  - `documents` (const std::vector< RetrievedChunk > &): All retrieved candidate documents.
  - `infer` (const InferenceFn &): LLM inference callback.
- Return: MultiStepRAGResult with final_answer and step details.
- Details: Splits documents into token-budget-sized batches, calls infer once per batch, then calls infer once more to synthesise the final answer from the partial results. If all documents fit in a single context window the single-pass path is taken and MultiStepRAGResult::context_overflow is false. query Original user query. documents All retrieved candidate documents. infer LLM inference callback. MultiStepRAGResult with final_answer and step details.

#### `RAGContextAssembler(const RAGContextAssemblerConfig &cfg={})`

#### `AssembledContext assemble(const std::vector< RetrievedChunk > &chunks, const std::string &system_prompt, const std::string &query) const`
- Source: `include/rag/rag_context_assembler.h`:171
- Brief: Assemble context chunks within the token budget.
- Parameters:
  - `chunks` (const std::vector< RetrievedChunk > &): Candidate retrieved chunks (may be empty).
  - `system_prompt` (const std::string &): System / instruction prompt (used for budget calculation).
  - `query` (const std::string &): User query (used for budget calculation).
- Return: AssembledContext with: chunks_used: selected chunks in relevance order tokens_used: estimated token count of assembled context tokens_remaining_for_response: guaranteed response budget was_truncated: true if the last chunk was truncated to fit
- Details: Deterministically selects a subset of chunks that fit within the context window while reserving tokens for the model's response. Algorithm Compute ContextWindowBudget from config + system_prompt + query. Sort chunks by relevance_score descending with deterministic tie-breaking (chunk_id, source, content). Greedily fill the available token budget in sorted order. Optionally truncate the last over-budget chunk if config_.allow_partial_chunk is true. Complexity Time: O(n log n) where n = chunks.size() Space: O(n) for result.chunks_used chunks Candidate retrieved chunks (may be empty). system_prompt System / instruction prompt (used for budget calculation). query User query (used for budget calculation). AssembledContext with: chunks_used: selected chunks in relevance order tokens_used: estimated token count of assembled context tokens_remaining_for_response: guaranteed response budget was_truncated: true if the last chunk was truncated to fit config_.model_context_tokens > 0 (or defaults to kDefaultContextWindowTokens) config_.min_response_tokens >= 0 chunks.empty() is valid; returns valid empty context result.tokens_remaining_for_response >= min_response_tokens result.tokens_remaining_for_response >= 20% of model_context_tokens result.tokens_used + result.tokens_remaining_for_response <= model_context_tokens result.chunks_used is sorted by relevance descending Thread-safe: no concurrent mutation of config_ during call Fail-closed: empty input or zero budget returns valid empty context

#### `int computeMaxTokens(const ContextWindowBudget &budget, int user_max=0)`
- Source: `include/rag/rag_context_assembler.h`:206
- Brief: Compute the recommended InferenceRequest::max_tokens value.
- Parameters:
  - `budget` (const ContextWindowBudget &): Input parameter.
  - `user_max` (int): Input parameter.
- Return: Recommended max_tokens value for InferenceRequest.
- Details: Compute Max Tokens. Ensures the model cannot be asked to generate more tokens than the context window can hold after the prompt is filled. This guards against token budget exhaustion during generation. Calculation Calculate available_response_tokens = budget.reserved_response_tokens If user_max > 0: clamp to min(user_max, available_response_tokens) Otherwise: use available_response_tokens Ensure result >= 1 (model must be able to generate at least one token) budget Pre-computed ContextWindowBudget for the request (from ContextWindowBudget::compute(context_window, system_prompt, query, min_response_tokens)). user_max Caller-supplied cap. If 0: unconstrained by caller. If >0: result is min(user_max, available_response). Recommended max_tokens value for InferenceRequest. return value >= 1 return value <= budget.reserved_response_tokens if user_max > 0: return value <= user_max Thread-safe: stateless function Complexity: O(1) budget Input parameter. user_max Input parameter. Return value.

#### `std::string buildEntityContext(const std::vector< ingestion::BaseEntity > &entities)`
- Source: `include/rag/rag_ingestion_bridge.h`:329

- Details: Build Entity Context. Formats the entity list as a single line for injection into an LLM prompt. Each entity is formatted as TYPE display_name:entity_id. Format Example"Extractedentities:LEGAL_PROVISIONlaw:BGB:§823\|ORGANIZATIONorg:abc12" Behavior Empty entity list returns empty string (no "Extracted entities:" prefix) Single entity: prefix + entity Multiple entities: prefix + entity1 \| entity2 \| ... Used by downstream re-rankers and prompt builders Complexity Time: O(n) where n = entities.size() Space: O(n) for output string entities Entities to summarise. Empty vector returns empty string. Formatted context string suitable for LLM prompt injection; empty string when entities is empty or contains no displayable entities. Complexity: O(n) where n = entities.size() Stateless function (no mutable state) Thread-safe: pure function, no concurrency issues Fail-closed: malformed entities result in skipping that entity, not error enrichRetrievedDocuments() uses this to populate metadata["_entities"] extractEntitiesForContext() to extract entities before calling this entities Input parameter. Return value.

#### `std::size_t enrichRetrievedDocuments(std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &docs)`
- Source: `include/rag/rag_ingestion_bridge.h`:267
- Brief: Enrich a list of retrieved documents with NER entity context.

- Return: Number of documents that received at least one entity. (Note: all documents may still be enriched with content/source metadata)
- Details: Enrich Retrieved Documents. ProcessingFor each document in docs: Canonicalizes retrieval metadata: Sets metadata["content"] from RetrievedDocument::content (if empty) Sets metadata["source"] from RetrievedDocument::id (if empty) Runs extractEntitiesForContext() on the document content If entities are found, appends a compact entity context string to metadata["_entities"] for downstream re-rankers and prompt builders Failure Handling (Fail-Closed)Documents without content or stable id are skipped (not modified). Documents that yield no entities still retain the canonical content/source metadata backfill. This operation is safe to call on an empty vector (no-op). Complexity Time: O(d * e) where d = docs.size(), e = entity extraction overhead Space: O(1) auxiliary (modifies docs in-place) docs Retrieved documents to enrich (modified in-place). Safe to pass empty vector (no-op). Number of documents that received at least one entity. (Note: all documents may still be enriched with content/source metadata) docs not null (vector, not pointer) docs[i].metadata["content"] is set if previously empty docs[i].metadata["source"] is set if previously empty docs[i].metadata["_entities"] is set if entities were found Complexity: O(d * e) where d = docs.size(), e = extraction overhead Thread-safe: each document processed independently Fail-closed: documents without content/id are silently skipped docs Input/output parameter. Return value.

#### `IndexResult indexDocument(const std::string &text, const std::string &collection="default", const std::string &mime="text/plain", const std::string &filename="document.txt")`
- Source: `include/rag/rag_ingestion_bridge.h`:201
- Brief: Index a document by running the full ingestion workflow and writing extracted chunks and entities to the configured sinks.
- Parameters:
  - `text` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `collection` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `mime` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `filename` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: IndexResult with: ok: true on success, false on failure (validation, I/O, etc.) doc_id: assigned document identifier (format: "<collection>/<hash>") collection: echoed collection name entity_count: number of NER entities extracted vector_count: number of vector chunks written error: human-readable error message when ok == false
- Details: Thread-Safe: captures local copies of member pointers before use. Workflow Validates input size (text <= kMaxDocumentChars, collection <= kMaxCollectionChars). Runs WorkflowEngine::execute() on the text to produce a BaseEntitySet (NER nodes, vector chunks, quality score). If workflow execution is unavailable or fails, a minimal fallback entity set is generated from direct entity extraction plus one canonical retrieval chunk so indexing remains usable. Writes all VectorRecord chunks to the IVectorWriter sink (if configured). Writes all BaseEntity nodes and EntityRelation edges to the IGraphWriter sink (if configured). Returns an IndexResult describing the outcome. IdempotencyThe returned doc_id has the form "<collection>/<doc_hash>" where doc_hash is a stable 16-character hex digest of the input text. Re-indexing the same text produces the same doc_id and overwrite previous records in the vector and graph sinks. text UTF-8 text to index. Empty text is rejected with IndexResult{.ok=false, .error="empty input"}. Text larger than kMaxDocumentChars (5 MiB) is rejected. collection Logical collection name (e.g. "legal-docs", "default"). Must be <= kMaxCollectionChars (256 chars). Default: "default". mime MIME type hint for workflow routing (e.g. "text/plain", "text/html"). Default: "text/plain". filename Filename hint for workflow routing (e.g. "document.txt"). Default: "document.txt". IndexResult with: ok: true on success, false on failure (validation, I/O, etc.) doc_id: assigned document identifier (format: "<collection>/<hash>") collection: echoed collection name entity_count: number of NER entities extracted vector_count: number of vector chunks written error: human-readable error message when ok == false toolbox must not be null (checked in constructor) text.size() <= kMaxDocumentChars (5 MiB) collection.size() <= kMaxCollectionChars (256 chars) if ok == true: doc_id is non-empty and deterministic from text if ok == false: error message is non-empty and human-readable vector_count reflects records written to vector_writer (0 if null) entity_count reflects records written to graph_writer (0 if null) Does not throw: empty text returns IndexResult{ok=false, error="empty input"} Complexity: O(m * e) where m = text.size(), e = extraction overhead Thread-safe: no mutable shared state modified Fail-closed: malformed input returns IndexResult.ok=false, not exception text Input parameter. collection Input parameter. mime Input parameter. filename Input parameter. Return value. Complexity: O(m * e) where m = text.size(), e = entity extraction overhead Failure modes handled (all return ok=false with descriptive error): - Empty text: rejected with error="empty input" - Text > kMaxDocumentChars (5 MiB): rejected with error="input too large" - Invalid collection name (empty, >256 chars, control chars): rejected - Invalid mime type (empty, >128 chars, control chars): rejected - Invalid filename (empty, >512 chars, control chars): rejected - Workflow engine unavailable: falls back to direct entity extraction (fail-open) - Vector writer failure: logged, partial result returned (vector_count=0) - Graph writer failure: logged, indexing continues without graph writes All validation errors are logged at WARN level; I/O errors at WARN level. Doc ID is deterministic from text hash (idempotent re-indexing). Fallback workflow ensures partial indexing succeeds even without full pipeline.

The formatted document contains: query_id, latency_ms, and the explain_plan_json snippet (first 2 048 chars to stay within chunk budget). query_id Stable query fingerprint / request-id. plan_json JSON-serialised EXPLAIN / BaoOptimizer plan. latency_ms Observed end-to-end query latency in milliseconds. collection Target collection (default: "optimizer-logs"). IndexResult from the underlying indexDocument() call. query_id Identifier of the query. plan_json Input parameter. latency_ms Input parameter. collection Input parameter. Return value.

#### `ReplugFusionResult fuse(const std::string &query, const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &candidates) const`
- Source: `include/rag/replug_retriever.h`:218
- Brief: Score and re-rank candidates by fusing retrieval and LLM scores.
- Parameters:
  - `query` (const std::string &): User query.
  - `candidates` (const std::vector< judge::RetrievedDocument > &): Retrieved candidate documents.
- Return: Fusion result with re-ranked documents and score breakdown.
- Details: Steps performed: Drop candidates with retrieval_score < config_.min_retrieval_score. Normalise retrieval scores to [0, 1]. Compute LLM scores for each candidate via the injected scorer. Apply softmax with config_.temperature to LLM raw scores. Interpolate: fused = (1-λ)*retrieval + λ*llm. Sort by fused score descending; truncate to config_.top_k. The similarity_score field of every returned document is set to the fused score. query User query. candidates Retrieved candidate documents. Fusion result with re-ranked documents and score breakdown.

#### `void updateRetrieverWeights(const ReplugFusionResult &result)`
- Source: `include/rag/replug_retriever.h`:239
- Brief: Apply one REPLUG-LSR gradient step to the per-document weight table using the KL-divergence gradient from a previous fuse().
- Parameters:
  - `result` (const ReplugFusionResult &): Input parameter.
- Details: Update Retriever Weights. For each document d: weight[d]+=lr*kl_gradient[d] weight[d]=clamp(weight[d],0,1) The updated weights are incorporated in subsequent fuse() calls by multiplying the retrieval score by weight[d] before normalisation. No-op when config_.enable_weight_update is false. result Result from the most recent fuse() call. result Input parameter. Calls: std::abs(), epsilon(), std::clamp().

#### `GuardrailDecision checkFederatedCost(const std::string &query, const FederatedQueryPlan &plan) const`
- Source: `include/rag/retrieval_guardrail.h`:139
- Brief: Evaluates whether plan should be executed given cost thresholds.
- Parameters:
  - `query` (const std::string &): The raw query string (UTF-8).
  - `plan` (const FederatedQueryPlan &): Federated plan to evaluate.
- Return: GuardrailDecision describing the allow/deny outcome.
- Details: Steps: If the guardrail is disabled, return allow unconditionally. Derive effective cost: use plan.estimated_cost_ms when non-zero, otherwise call TensorRagCostModel::estimate() with default config adapted to plan.num_chunks. Select threshold: max_cross_dc_cost_ms for cross-DC plans, max_cost_ms otherwise. Deny with structured reason and THEMIS_WARN when cost > threshold. query The raw query string (UTF-8). plan Federated plan to evaluate. GuardrailDecision describing the allow/deny outcome.

  - `query_confidence` (double): Input parameter.
- Return: SelfRAGResult with graded passages and per-round stats.

Otherwise increment round counter and re-retrieve (up to max_rounds). query Natural-language query. query_confidence Confidence override passed to shouldRetrieve(). - Details: ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── WikiIndexStore::addVector — [W8-18] hnswlib wiring + exhaustive fallback ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── [W8-18] Stores the vector in the in-memory HNSW injection-bridge backend if WikiIndexStoreConfig::enable_hnsw is true. When the backend is disabled (compile-time flag THEMIS_HNSW_BACKEND not set or enable_hnsw is false) the call is a no-op and logs a warning. doc_id Document identifier (must match an already-indexed document to allow hybrid fusion). embedding Dense vector; all calls must use the same dimension. std::invalid_argument if embedding is empty. doc_id Identifier of the doc. embedding Input parameter.

#### `std::vector< IndexResult > searchHybrid(const std::vector< std::string > &query_terms, const std::vector< float > &query_embedding, size_t top_k=10) const`
- Source: `include/rag/wiki_index_store.h`:279
- Brief: Hybrid BM25+ + HNSW search fused with RRF.
- Parameters:
  - `query_terms` (const std::vector< std::string > &): Tokenised BM25+ query.
  - `query_embedding` (const std::vector< float > &): Dense query vector for HNSW (may be empty when HNSW is disabled; caller must not mix empty and populated embeddings across add/search calls).
  - `top_k` (size_t): Maximum results.
- Return: Fused results, descending score.
- Details: [W8-21] Runs BM25+ lexical search on query_terms and HNSW approximate nearest-neighbour search on query_embedding in parallel, then fuses both ranked lists with fuseRRF(). When HNSW is disabled (config or compile flag) only the BM25+ list is used — the result is identical to searchBM25(). query_terms Tokenised BM25+ query. query_embedding Dense query vector for HNSW (may be empty when HNSW is disabled; caller must not mix empty and populated embeddings across add/search calls). top_k Maximum results. Fused results, descending score.

#### `std::vector< IndexResult > searchPhrase(const std::string &phrase, size_t top_k=10) const`
- Source: `include/rag/wiki_index_store.h`:155
- Brief: Exact phrase search using positional index.
- Parameters:
  - `phrase` (const std::string &): Raw phrase string.
  - `top_k` (size_t): Maximum results to return.
- Return: Ranked results, descending score.
- Details: Tokenises phrase with the same whitespace tokeniser used during indexing, then returns documents where the terms appear as a consecutive run (pos[i+1] == pos[i]+1 for every adjacent pair). Results are BM25+-scored and sorted descending. Edge cases: Single-term phrase → equivalent to searchBM25. Empty phrase → returns empty vector. phrase Raw phrase string. top_k Maximum results to return. Ranked results, descending score.

#### `std::vector< IndexResult > searchProximity(const std::string &term1, const std::string &term2, size_t distance, size_t top_k=10) const`
- Source: `include/rag/wiki_index_store.h`:178
- Brief: Proximity search: docs where term1 and term2 are ≤ distance apart.
- Parameters:
  - `term1` (const std::string &): First term (pre-tokenised, lowercase).
  - `term2` (const std::string &): Second term (pre-tokenised, lowercase).
  - `distance` (size_t): Maximum allowed token distance (inclusive).
  - `top_k` (size_t): Maximum results to return.
- Return: Ranked results, descending score.
- Details: Uses the positional index to find the minimum token-distance between any occurrence of term1 and any occurrence of term2 within the same document. Only documents satisfying the distance constraint are returned. Edge cases: term1 == term2 → requires ≥2 occurrences whose mutual distance satisfies the constraint. Either term absent in corpus → returns empty vector. term1 First term (pre-tokenised, lowercase). term2 Second term (pre-tokenised, lowercase). distance Maximum allowed token distance (inclusive). top_k Maximum results to return. Ranked results, descending score.

#### `ReconstructionScore evaluate(const std::string &original, const std::string &recovered) const override`
- Source: `include/rag/delegate_evaluator.h`:414
- Brief: Evaluate AQL query fidelity via Jaccard similarity.
- Parameters:
  - `original` (const std::string &): The original AQL string.
  - `recovered` (const std::string &): The recovered AQL string.
- Return: RS in [0.0, 1.0].
- Details: original The original AQL string. recovered The recovered AQL string. RS in [0.0, 1.0]. Empty-input convention: when both original and recovered tokenise to the empty set (e.g. whitespace/punctuation-only input), this function returns 0.0. This intentionally deviates from the strict set-theory definition where Jaccard(∅, ∅) = 1.0: an empty token set carries no lexical content, so treating it as "perfect reconstruction" would be semantically misleading for the RS metric.

#### `DomainType domain() const override`

- Parameters: none

#### `ReconstructionScore evaluate(const std::string &original, const std::string &recovered) const override`
- Source: `include/rag/delegate_evaluator.h`:446
- Brief: Evaluate plain-text fidelity via normalised edit distance.
- Parameters:
  - `original` (const std::string &): The original text.
  - `recovered` (const std::string &): The recovered text.
- Return: RS in [0.0, 1.0].
- Details: original The original text. recovered The recovered text. RS in [0.0, 1.0]. Large-string approximation: for inputs exceeding 10 000 characters the underlying editDistance() switches from the exact O(n×m) Levenshtein DP to an O(n) Hamming-style approximation (aligned-prefix character differences + length delta). This keeps RS computation under ~5 ms for 100 KB inputs at the cost of reduced accuracy when the edit distance is large relative to the string length. For document payloads beyond this size, consider using XmlProcessEvaluator or JsonDocumentEvaluator instead.

Return value must not be discarded; the function silently clamps out-of-range k to the last available index. No exception is thrown for out-of-range k.

- Source: `include/rag/delegate_evaluator.h`:315
- Brief: Run the round-trip relay.

When num_round_trips == 0 the function returns immediately with RS@0 = 1.0 and StopReason::COMPLETED_NORMALLY. seed_doc Original document before any edits. edit_pairs Ordered list of edit pair instructions. Must not be empty unless num_round_trips == 0. evaluator Domain-specific scorer used after each backward edit. edit_fn Callable that applies a single edit instruction to a document. Must be non-null. RelayResult containing full RS history and metadata. std::invalid_argument If edit_pairs is empty and num_round_trips > 0, or if edit_fn is null. seed_doc Input parameter. edit_pairs Input parameter. evaluator Input parameter. edit_fn Input parameter. Return value.

#### `bool isReady() const`
- Source: `include/rag/nli_faithfulness_verifier.h`:199
- Brief: Check if verifier is ready for inference.
- Parameters: none
- Return: true if the verifier can perform inference
- Details: Returns true when at least one inference path is available: ONNX model is loaded (isModelLoaded() is true), or ONNX is disabled (Config::use_onnx == false), so heuristic runs unconditionally, or ONNX is enabled but the heuristic fallback is also enabled (Config::fallback_to_heuristic == true). Returns false only when ONNX is required (use_onnx == true) and no fallback is permitted (fallback_to_heuristic == false) and no model has been loaded yet. true if the verifier can perform inference

- Source: `include/rag/knowledge_graph_retriever.h`:305
- Brief: BFS neighbourhood starting from start_id.
- Parameters:
  - `start_id` (const std::string &): Starting node identifier.
  - `max_depth` (size_t): Maximum BFS depth (1 = direct neighbours only).
  - `min_edge_weight` (double): Minimum edge weight to follow.
  - `max_nodes` (size_t): Maximum number of nodes to visit (DoS guard, GAP-010). The BFS terminates once this many nodes have been enqueued, preventing unbounded traversal of dense graphs. Defaults to 4096.
- Return: Set of reachable node IDs (excluding start_id).
- Details: Returns all nodes reachable within max_depth hops over edges whose weight is at least min_edge_weight. start_id Starting node identifier. max_depth Maximum BFS depth (1 = direct neighbours only). min_edge_weight Minimum edge weight to follow. max_nodes Maximum number of nodes to visit (DoS guard, GAP-010). The BFS terminates once this many nodes have been enqueued, preventing unbounded traversal of dense graphs. Defaults to 4096. Set of reachable node IDs (excluding start_id).

#### `void setLlmSampleFn(LlmSampleFn fn)`
- Source: `include/rag/knowledge_gap_detector.h`:376
- Brief: Inject an LLM-based sample generator for the self-consistency check.
- Parameters:
  - `fn` (LlmSampleFn): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Llm Sample Fn. When set, checkSelfConsistency() calls this function to obtain answer candidates instead of cycling document sentences. Pass an empty (default-constructed) function to revert to the heuristic path. fn LlmSampleFn — receives (query, num_samples) and returns candidates. fn Input parameter. Calls: std::move().

#### `void setRetrievalCallback(RetrievalCallback fn)`
- Source: `include/rag/knowledge_gap_detector.h`:365
- Brief: Set the retrieval callback used by the FLARE active-retrieval loop.
- Parameters:
  - `fn` (RetrievalCallback): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Retrieval Callback. When set, detectWithActiveRetrieval() calls this function each time the current document set does not provide sufficient coverage and a reformulated sub-query is available. The callback must be thread-safe with respect to the surrounding context but does not need to be re-entrant. Passing a default-constructed (empty) function disables dynamic retrieval; the FLARE loop will then exit after the initial document set check without attempting further searches. fn RetrievalCallback — receives (query, k) and returns documents. fn Input parameter. Calls: std::move().

- Brief: Get the current optimized retrieval parameters.
- Parameters: none
- Return: Current optimized retrieval parameters (thread-safe read).
- Details: Returns the retrieval parameters most recently updated by the adaptive optimization loop. Callers should apply these parameters when issuing retrieval requests so that the system benefits from online learning. The parameters are updated every time triggerLearningIteration() runs and sufficient feedback (both user signals and evaluation confidence scores) has been collected. Until the first optimization cycle the method returns the default values from RetrievalParams. Current optimized retrieval parameters (thread-safe read).

This is called automatically during runLoRARetraining() and can also be invoked directly for manual testing or scheduled jobs. adapter_id Adapter being retrained (used for logging). candidate_samples Raw samples to run the pipeline on. current_metrics Monitoring metrics for adaptive threshold adjustment. Selection result (selected samples + audit provenance).

#### `void setFeedbackEntryCountProvider(std::function< size_t()> provider)`
- Source: `include/rag/continuous_learning_orchestrator.h`:441
- Brief: Inject a FeedbackCollector new-entry-count provider for Loop 4.
- Parameters:
  - `provider` (std::function< size_t()>): n/a
- Details: The callback should return the number of new feedback entries since the last Loop-4 run. Loop 4 only commits a new adapter when the count reaches ≥ 100. Without a provider the guardrail falls back to the accuracy-proxy heuristic. Roadmap ref: src/rag/ROADMAP.md §Phase 8 Loop 4

#### `void setHnswMissRateProvider(std::function< double()> provider)`
- Source: `include/rag/continuous_learning_orchestrator.h`:418
- Brief: Inject a BaoOptimizer miss-rate provider for Loop 1 guardrail checks.
- Parameters:
  - `provider` (std::function< double()>): n/a
- Details: The callback should return the current HNSW query miss-rate in [0.0, 1.0]. Loop 1's guardrail passes when the returned value is below 0.05. Without a provider the guardrail falls back to the accuracy-proxy heuristic. Roadmap ref: src/rag/ROADMAP.md §Phase 8 Loop 1

#### `void wireLiveSignalProviders(std::shared_ptr< themis::performance::phase3::BaoOptimizer > bao_optimizer, std::shared_ptr< themis::performance::WorkloadAdaptiveOptimizer > workload_optimizer, std::shared_ptr< themis::prompt_engineering::FeedbackCollector > feedback_collector)`

  - `feedback_collector` (std::shared_ptr< themis::prompt_engineering::FeedbackCollector >): Input parameter.
- Throws:
  - std::runtime_error: if an error occurs.
- Details: Wire Live Signal Providers. This bootstrap helper binds Loop-1/2/4 signals to: BaoOptimizer::getMissRate() WorkloadAdaptiveOptimizer::getProfileDrift() FeedbackCollector::newEntryCount() Internally weak references are used, so providers fail closed (with warning + fallback) if a dependency is released during runtime. bao_optimizer Input parameter. workload_optimizer Input parameter. feedback_collector Input parameter. std::runtime_error if an error occurs. Calls: defined(), setHnswMissRateProvider(), lock(), spdlog::warn(), getMissRate(), setWorkloadDriftProvider(), getProfileDrift(), setFeedbackEntryCountProvider().

- Return: Full multi-hop reasoning result.
- Details: Decomposes the query into sub-questions, runs one retrieval + inference hop per sub-question (injecting previous partial answers as context), then composes a final answer from all intermediate results. When either retrieval_fn or inference_fn is null the method returns an empty MultiHopResult immediately. query Original user query. retrieval_fn Callable that retrieves documents for a sub-query. inference_fn Callable that generates text from a prompt. Full multi-hop reasoning result.

#### `RLAIFTrainingStep runTrainingStep(const std::string &query, const std::string &draft_response)`
- Source: `include/rag/rlaif_trainer.h`:346
- Brief: Run one constitutional AI + preference generation step.
- Parameters:
  - `query` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `draft_response` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: Training step result including the preference pair.
- Details: Run Training Step. Steps performed: Apply critique-revision cycles to draft_response. After all iterations, call the judge on (draft, final_revision). If judge prefers the revision, create a (query, revision, draft) preference pair; otherwise (query, draft, revision). Store the pair in the internal dataset when preference_score >= config_.min_preference_score. query The user query that generated draft_response. draft_response The initial (potentially low-quality) response. Training step result including the preference pair. query Input parameter. draft_response Input parameter. Return value. Calls: std::chrono::steady_clock::now(), empty(), lock(), applyRevisionCycle(), push_back(), createPreferencePair(), size(), count().
